```
SCREEN: U05-add-sheet (inside U05)
USER GOAL(S): Enter a cash purchase quickly; Enter cash received; Pick account and category; Cancel without losing work
ELEMENT COUNT: 10 interactive: Spent + Received segment buttons (aria-pressed), amount input (text/decimal, aria-label dynamic), date input (native), account select, category select (optgroup), description input (maxLength 500, 'Optional'), submit 'Add transaction' (primary, 46px, right-aligned), sheet Close X (44px top-right), scrim. All above the fold with keyboard closed (est. ~560px of 743px max). Hidden direction input is not interactive.
HEURISTIC NOTE: Strong vocabulary and minimalism (no member picker, description optional, sign handled by a Spent/Received toggle rather than a minus). Weak on efficiency and control: no autofocus, no remembered account, draft lost on any dismiss, no undo, submit at the bottom of a keyboard-covered sheet.
FINDINGS: U05:submit-under-keyboard, U05:amount-not-autofocused, U05:scrim-discards-entry, U05:sheet-no-swipe-dismiss, U05:no-save-confirmation-undo, U05:linked-accounts-in-manual-add, U05:account-default-not-last-used, U05:fab-navigates-away, U05:no-new-category-in-add-sheet, U05:silent-noop-without-accounts, U05:field-label-10px, U05:category-optgroup-duplicate, U05:submit-label-shift (see parent block)
```

ELEMENT COUNT: Same 10 elements plus one role=alert banner (add-form.tsx:137) rendered between the fields and the submit; submit re-enabled. No per-field error, no aria-invalid / aria-describedby.
HEURISTIC NOTE: H9 weak: generic banner, wording 'Amount is required.' for malformed amounts, and uncontrolled fields likely reset while the error shows.

```
SCREEN: U05-add-sheet-offline (inside U05)
USER GOAL(S): Save a cash entry while offline and trust it will land
ELEMENT COUNT: Same elements; an ink toast 'You're offline. We'll retry when you're back.' (run-action.ts:16) appears above the tab bar with a Dismiss button; form stays filled and the sheet stays open (add-form.tsx:46 returns prev). On reconnect a leaf toast 'Back online - transaction added.' shows and the sheet closes (add-form.tsx:41-43).
HEURISTIC NOTE: Best-handled state in the unit: clear status, no data loss, single retry. Note the sheet remains open and blocks the app until reconnect or manual close (closing discards the draft while a retry is still queued, and a later reconnect will still submit it - the user cannot see or cancel the queued write).
FINDINGS: none beyond the parent block

Inline error 'Enter a name.' is adjacent to the fields.

```
SCREEN: categories-add-form (inside U08)
USER GOAL(S): Add a top-level category; Add a child category under an existing parent; Optionally set a report code
ELEMENT COUNT: 4 controls stacked single-column on mobile (Name text, Code text, Parent native select, Add primary); all above the fold; Code hint 'Optional, auto-filled'.
HEURISTIC NOTE: Good: required Name, focus returns to Name after add, 16px inputs, 44px targets. Weak: error placement, parent reset, no success feedback, workbook 'Code' vocabulary.
FINDINGS: U08:parent-select-resets-after-add, U08:add-error-below-form-under-keyboard, U08:inputs-no-enterkeyhint-autocomplete, U08:field-label-10-5px, U08:new-child-hidden-in-collapsed-parent (see parent block)
```

```
SCREEN: categories-active-list (inside U08)
USER GOAL(S): See categories and nesting; Open a row to rename; Archive a row
ELEMENT COUNT: Per top-level row: 44x44 expand toggle (only if it has children), 'Edit' and 'Archive' 12px text buttons; children indented under the parent when expanded with the same two actions. 'N top-level' caption plus 'Show archived (n)' toggle above the list.
HEURISTIC NOTE: Parents collapsed by default with an 11px glyph; destructive action adjacent to Edit; codes at 11px mono.
FINDINGS: U08:collapsed-parents-no-child-count, U08:row-actions-small-adjacent-destructive, U08:top-level-counter-noise (see parent block)
```

```
SCREEN: categories-row-edit (inside U08)
USER GOAL(S): Rename a category; Change its report code
ELEMENT COUNT: Inline replacement of the row: Name input (autoFocus), Code input (required, pattern), Save primary, Cancel ghost; stacked on mobile.
HEURISTIC NOTE: Save shows 'Saving…' but server errors are swallowed; Code mandatory here though optional on add; no Esc to cancel.
FINDINGS: U08:rename-silent-failure, U08:code-required-on-edit, U08:inputs-no-enterkeyhint-autocomplete (see parent block)
```

```
SCREEN: categories-archive-confirm-sheet (inside U08)
USER GOAL(S): Confirm or cancel archiving a category
ELEMENT COUNT: Bottom sheet via portal: scrim (tap to cancel), decorative handle, h2 prompt naming the category, description of what is hidden and kept, Cancel (46px pill) and Archive (46px destructive pill) stacked column-reverse so Archive is lowest.
HEURISTIC NOTE: Copy names exactly what is lost (good). Enter-anywhere confirms; no swipe dismiss despite handle; no undo after.
FINDINGS: U08:confirm-enter-overrides-cancel, U08:sheet-handle-no-swipe, U08:archive-no-undo-no-progress (see parent block)
```

```
SCREEN: categories-archived-view (inside U08)
USER GOAL(S): Find an archived category; Restore it
ELEMENT COUNT: '← Back to active' toggle, list of archived parents plus active parents that own archived children, 'Unarchive' form button per archived row ('Restoring…' while pending), EmptyState 'Nothing archived' when empty.
HEURISTIC NOTE: Client-only toggle (aria-pressed) so switching is instant; active parents keep live Edit/Archive here.
FINDINGS: U08:archived-view-mixes-active-parents (see parent block)
```

```
SCREEN: categories-empty (inside U08)
USER GOAL(S): Understand what a category is and add the first one
ELEMENT COUNT: EmptyState with explanatory body and one primary 'Add a category' button that scrolls to and focuses #new-category (the form is already above it).
HEURISTIC NOTE: The CTA duplicates the visible form directly above it; harmless but redundant. Reached from /budgets only in the zero-category state.
FINDINGS: U08:no-entry-once-categories-exist (see parent block)
```

```
SCREEN: /rules (U09 rules)
USER GOAL(S): See which 'always do this' rules exist and whether each one is actually doing something; Create a rule (from scratch here, or prefilled via 'Always share' on a transaction row) so future matching transactions are shared / categorised / recorded as a payment automatically; Edit, reorder, pause or resume an existing rule; Re-run a rule over the last 12 months; Delete a rule and decide whether the shares it created are rolled back
ELEMENT COUNT: 36 (primary 4, secondary 29, destructive 3, above fold 9) [code-estimate]
TAPS TO PRIMARY GOAL: 4 (scrolls 1) - Cold open /dashboard -> More (1) -> Rules (2) -> New rule (3) -> type merchant, review ~9 fields, scroll the sheet -> Create rule (4). The intended shorter path is J3: Transactions -> expand row (1) -> Always share (2) -> Create rule (3), which is 3 taps only if the user accepts the prefilled ±10% amount band and household split without changing anything. Rules sits in the More sheet by default (shell.tsx:44, group 'split'), so /rules itself is 2 taps from any main screen.
HEURISTIC SCORE: 11/20 (H1=1 H2=1 H3=1 H4=1 H5=1 H6=2 H7=1 H8=0 H9=1 H10=2)

FINDINGS

SCREEN: /shared (+ ?month ?account ?period); /settlements -> redirect to /shared (U10 shared)
USER GOAL(S): See who owes whom right now (running tally) and by how much; Flag a transaction on an account as shared (J9) and adjust the split; Confirm or dismiss an e-Transfer the ledger found as a payment; Settle a closed statement in one tap (J10) or record a partial/cash payment by hand; Close the period early; Review past periods and delete/unmatch a recorded payment; Bulk share / unshare everything on an account for a month
ELEMENT COUNT: 42 (primary 8, secondary 25, destructive 9, above fold 3) [code-estimate (typical state: 2 members, open period with lines, 1 awaiting statement, 1 payment prompt, 5 transactions on the selected account all shared, 2 past periods each with 1 payment; per-row = share toggle + Edit split + Clear + Always share/Manage rules; above the fold at 390x844 = Close period now, Detect e-Transfers, Mark settled)]
TAPS TO PRIMARY GOAL: 2 (scrolls 3) - Cold /dashboard: /shared is NOT on the default tab bar (shell.tsx:62 DEFAULT_TABS = dashboard/transactions/budgets), so it is More (1) -> Shared (2). Who-owes-whom is then answered with 0 scrolls (OpenPeriodCard is the first block, page.tsx:355). Sharing a grocery row (J9) is 2 taps + ~3 scrolls past running tally, nudge, awaiting card, payment prompts, shared-with-you, switcher+bulk card, 3 stat tiles and MonthNav (page.tsx:355-433) + optionally 2 taps to change Source account (native select, page.tsx:303 defaults to the first credit card, not last used) + 1 tap on the row checkbox = 3-5 taps, 3 scrolls, 2 decisions (which account, which row). Over the 3-tap / 2-decision budget. Settling a closed statement (J10) is 2 taps + 1 tap on Mark settled with no confirm.
HEURISTIC SCORE: 11/20 (H1=1 H2=1 H3=1 H4=1 H5=1 H6=1 H7=1 H8=1 H9=1 H10=2)

The page's own 'Outstanding now: $42.11' headline never updated either. I only found out the payment had actually gone through - it had, on the very first tap - by force-reloading the page and seeing 'All square'. | Settling up is the entire point of this screen. A payment that silently succeeds server-side but looks hung/failed client-side, with no toast and no visible success state, forces the user to abandon the page and reload just to learn whether their money was recorded. On a real device with no devtools to check, a person would either sit staring at a frozen 'Saving…' not knowing whether to wait, force-quit, or worry the payment vanished - none of which match what actually happened (it worked instantly). | In src/app/(app)/shared/record-form.tsx the form binds React's useActionState directly to <form action={formAction}> (lines 30-34); every other action on this page (Close period at period-card.tsx, Mark as not shared / Share all unflagged at bulk-actions.tsx) instead uses useTransition + startTransition and correctly returns to an idle state after the server responds. Reproduce with devtools open, submit the form, and trace why `pending` never flips back to false despite the action resolving with {ok:true} at actions.ts:296; the existing success message ('Payment recorded', record-form.tsx:107-111) is fully coded but never gets the chance to render. Add a client-side timeout/fallback so a pending state that hasn't cleared within ~5s surfaces an error/retry affordance instead of hanging forever. To actually review which of the household's transactions are and aren't flagged shared, the owner has to click through up to 17 separate account filters one by one - the default selection is just the first credit-card-type account returned by the query, not anything meaningfully relevant. | The product model says the user never wants a separate review/audit step; browsing 'what's shared so far' by paging through 17 individual account filters is exactly that kind of manual audit, on a page whose whole promise is 'split fairly, settle quickly.' | Add an 'All accounts' entry as the default option in the Source account combobox that unions transactions across every account (Joint and Mine) into one list with the same Share/Unshare controls, and default the switcher to it instead of the first credit-card account by query order. USER GOAL(S): See this month's income, expenses and net at a glance; See where the money went this month (top categories) and how big each share is; Compare months across the year and jump to a past month; Drill from a category into the transactions behind it; Check year-to-date position
ELEMENT COUNT: 15 (primary 0, secondary 15, destructive 0, above fold 15) [code-estimate]
TAPS TO PRIMARY GOAL: 2 (scrolls 0) - From cold /dashboard: P&L is not on the default tab bar (shell.tsx:62 DEFAULT_TABS = dashboard/transactions/budgets), so it is More (1) -> Profit & Loss (2). Month tiles are visible with 0 scrolls once the page loads. Interactive elements: MonthNav prev/next (2, month-nav.tsx:31-53), 'This month' link (1, only when off the current month, month-nav.tsx:63-68), 12 chart month links (page.tsx:178-202). Empty state adds 1 primary CTA 'Import transactions' (page.tsx:120-124). Category rows are not interactive. Fold estimate at 390x844: top bar ~56 + py-5 + MonthNav 44 + tiles ~65 + chart card ~320 = ~550px, so the first 2-3 category rows also sit above the fold; the 12-column chart itself is 440px wide inside a ~318px inner width so months Oct-Dec are off-screen to the right.
HEURISTIC SCORE: 11/20 (H1=1 H2=1 H3=2 H4=1 H5=1 H6=1 H7=1 H8=2 H9=0 H10=1)

Plaid-linked households will have card payments, e-transfers between own accounts, and refunds in every month. | Exclude inter-account movements from both series: (a) add an `is_transfer boolean` (or `kind` enum) column on transactions set by the Plaid sync when Plaid reports a transfer category or when a matching opposite-sign transaction exists in another household account within 3 days; (b) in page.tsx:39-43 add `.eq('is_transfer', false)`; (c) treat negative amounts that carry an expense category (refunds) as negative expense rather than income at page.tsx:67-68. ```
SCREEN: pnl:loaded (inside U11)
USER GOAL(S): Read this month's income / expenses / net; See which categories dominated the month; Scan the year and jump to another month
ELEMENT COUNT: MonthNav prev/next (2 x 44px) + conditional 'This month' link + 12 chart month links (~31px wide, 140px+ tall). No primary CTA; no inputs; category rows static. All above the fold except the lower category rows. source: code-estimate.
HEURISTIC NOTE: Period is stated twice (MonthNav label, 'Top categories · <month>') and the chart card carries '<year> · monthly' - good for J11. Legend maps colour to Income/Expense, category rows carry names inline (no separate legend needed). Weakest on H1/H9 (numbers that do not reconcile, no value labels) and H4 (breakpoint and wording drift).
FINDINGS: U11:transfers-counted-as-income-expense, U11:uncategorized-missing-from-breakdown, U11:chart-no-values-without-tap, U11:chart-horizontal-scroll-no-affordance, U11:category-rows-not-drillable, U11:bar-width-vs-share-mismatch, U11:compact-tiles-lose-precision-and-sign, U11:month-nav-top-placement, U11:wording-inconsistent-and-jargon, U11:series-colour-only, U11:chart-bar-target-narrow, U11:top-12-cap-unstated, U11:sub-16px-text, U11:ytd-baseline-unstated (see parent block)
```

```
SCREEN: /balance-sheet (+ ?month=YYYY-MM-01) (U12 balance-sheet)
USER GOAL(S): See net worth, total assets and total liabilities as of a month with zero taps; See each account's balance and confirm it matches the bank (linked) without typing anything; Look at a previous month's balance sheet; Record a balance for a manual (cash / unsupported bank) account for the month; Notice when a number is wrong and get to the place that fixes it
ELEMENT COUNT: 4 (primary 1, secondary 3, destructive 0, above fold 4) [code-estimate]
TAPS TO PRIMARY GOAL: 2 (scrolls 0) - Cold open /dashboard -> More (bottom tab bar) -> Balance sheet = 2 taps, 0 decisions; net worth / assets / liabilities tiles are above the fold (page.tsx:190-214). Balance sheet is in the 'reports' group so it is never on the 3-slot tab bar by default (shell.tsx:47). Manual balance entry: + Update balances (3rd tap) + horizontal scroll inside the sheet to reach the input column + type + Save (4th tap) = 4 taps, 1 vertical scroll, 1 horizontal scroll per row. Previous month: 3 taps (‹ in MonthNav).
HEURISTIC SCORE: 7/20 (H1=0 H2=1 H3=1 H4=1 H5=0 H6=1 H7=1 H8=1 H9=0 H10=1)

FINDINGS
[P1] Update balances sheet: <input name="bal:{id}"> rows for Plaid-linked accounts | Every account, including every bank-linked one, appears in the Update balances sheet with an editable balance field. The user is invited to type balances the bank already reports. If they do, the next sync overwrites the value silently (plaid-sync.ts:545-547), so what they typed vanishes with no explanation. | The product model says balances arrive from Plaid and the user never types one. The page query does not even select plaid_account_id (page.tsx:43), so neither the ledger nor the form can tell linked from manual. Hand-typed values on linked accounts are wasted work that the sync then clobbers, which reads as the app losing data. | In page.tsx:43 add plaid_account_id to the accounts select and pass is_linked into formAccounts (page.tsx:139-152). In form.tsx:126-134 render linked rows as read-only text (the derived balance, with a 'Bank' pill and the item's last_synced_at) instead of an <input>; only manual rows keep the input. If a household has zero manual accounts, do not render the Update balances button at all (panel.tsx:25) and instead show a single secondary 'Refresh from bank' action that calls refreshPlaidBalances. (src/app/(app)/balance-sheet/page.tsx:43, src/app/(app)/balance-sheet/form.tsx:126) [code]
[P1] Ledger rows (page.tsx:300-310) and stat tiles (page.tsx:190-214) | Each account shows a name, 'Mine/Joint' and a dollar figure. Nothing says whether that figure came from the bank, was typed by hand, or is opening balance plus transactions. There is no 'last synced' anywhere on the page. A user who suspects a number is off cannot tell whether to wait for a sync, re-link, or type something. | Trust: a balance sheet that cannot say where its numbers come from is a balance sheet the user will verify against the bank app instead, and then stop opening. The product model makes linked-vs-manual the central distinction and the page has no marker for it. | Select plaid_account_id and join plaid_items.last_synced_at in the page query (page.tsx:40-57). In GroupRows (page.tsx:303-304) replace the second line with '{ownerLabel} · Bank, synced {relative time}' for linked rows and '{ownerLabel} · Manual, as of {snapshot month}' (or '· Manual, from transactions' when no snapshot) for manual rows. Under the summary bar add one line: 'Bank balances synced {time}' with a link to /accounts when any item is in an error state. (src/app/(app)/balance-sheet/page.tsx:303, src/app/(app)/balance-sheet/page.tsx:43) [code]
[P1] Update balances sheet: balance input + saveBalances | The sheet says 'Enter each account's balance as of August 2026'. The user types today's balance from their statement, taps Save, the sheet shows 'Saved.' and closes, and the ledger shows a different number than they just typed. | A snapshot dated YYYY-MM-01 is treated by the engine as the balance at the start of the month; every transaction dated the 2nd through month end is applied on top (balances.ts:86-105, documented in plaid-balances.ts:16-24). The Plaid path rolls the current balance back to the 1st to avoid this (plaid-balances.ts:104-113); the manual path does not, so any account with transactions this month shows typed value minus (or plus) this month's activity. 'The number I typed is not the number shown' destroys trust in the whole sheet. | In actions.ts:33-35, after parsing cents, roll the value back to the 1st exactly as snapshotsFromPlaidBalances does: fetch this month's transactions for the manual account ids (occurred_on > month and <= today) and store cents + rollback. ```
SCREEN: balance-sheet:update-balances (inside U12)
USER GOAL(S): Record this month's balance for a manual (cash / unsupported bank) account; Correct a wrong balance for one account; See last month's figure and the change while typing
ELEMENT COUNT: code-estimate: 1 primary (Save balances, bottom of scrolling body, not sticky), 1 secondary (Close X, top-right of sheet, 44x44), N text inputs inputMode=decimal (one per account, ALL accounts including linked, third column of a 560px-wide table so off-screen at 390px), scrim (dismiss). No destructive control, but blank input on Save deletes that month's snapshot. Above the fold when opened: sheet title, Close, intro paragraph, first section header, roughly first 2-3 rows with account name + prev-month column; the input column is not in view.
HEURISTIC NOTE: H1 0 (false 'Saved.'), H2 1 ('snapshot', 'opening balance'), H3 1 (dismissal discards, no undo for clears), H4 1 (subtotal differs from page), H5 0 (blank deletes, invalid dropped, linked editable, double-count), H6 0 (input column and its header off-screen), H7 1 (horizontal scroll per row, no per-row save), H8 1 (4 columns where 1 input is needed), H9 0 (no error UI), H10 1 (no sign guidance).
FINDINGS: U12:linked-accounts-editable, U12:typed-balance-double-counted, U12:prefill-is-anchor-not-balance, U12:form-table-horizontal-scroll, U12:false-saved-no-errors, U12:blank-clears-snapshot, U12:sheet-dismiss-loses-entries, U12:save-not-sticky, U12:sheet-subtotal-mismatch, U12:technical-copy-in-sheet, U12:liability-sign-unstated, U12:enter-submits-midway, U12:sheet-no-swipe-dismiss, U12:change-column-colour-only (see parent block)
```

```
SCREEN: /net-worth (U13 net-worth)
USER GOAL(S): See current household net worth at a glance; See how net worth has moved over the last two years and read the value at a past month; See the assets vs liabilities split and understand what is in each; Confirm the figure reflects the bank's balances after a Plaid sync (J11/J12)
ELEMENT COUNT: 3 (primary 1, secondary 2, destructive 0, above fold 3) [code-estimate]
TAPS TO PRIMARY GOAL: 2 (scrolls 0) - Cold open /dashboard -> More (bottom bar, shell.tsx:583-596) -> 'Net worth' tile in Reports group (shell.tsx:48, 685-699) = 2 taps, 0 scrolls, 1 decision. The dashboard already shows the same headline number and scrubbable chart with 0 taps (dashboard/client.tsx:261-401), and that hero does not link to /net-worth, so this screen's unique value (assets/liabilities split, YoY line) costs 2 taps through a sheet. Hero card ~345px tall; the two tiles land at roughly y=490-565 on 390x844, so everything is above the fold with the tab bar present. Elements: chart scrub gesture (secondary, hero.tsx:114-126); pull-to-sync gesture inherited from shell (secondary, shell.tsx:531); empty-state CTA button (primary, page.tsx:94-98, only in the empty state).
HEURISTIC SCORE: 12/20 (H1=1 H2=2 H3=1 H4=1 H5=2 H6=1 H7=1 H8=2 H9=0 H10=1)

FINDINGS
[P2] Trend chart SVG in NetWorthHero | A sighted user sees a green curve with faint gridlines and a dashed baseline but no month labels and no dollar values on the gridlines; they cannot tell whether the chart spans 6 months or 2 years, or what the swing between its low and high is worth, without scrubbing every point. | Only the aria-label (hero.tsx:119) says 'last 24 months'; the gridlines (hero.tsx:135-145) and zero line (hero.tsx:147-155) carry no text. A chart with no period and no scale fails the 'every number answers what is this' trust check and forces recall. | Below the <svg> add a 3-label row (flex justify-between, text-[11px] text-ink-3): monthLabel(trail[0].month), monthLabel(trail[12].month), 'Today' - short form e.g. 'Sep 2024'. Add <text> at the right edge of the top and bottom gridlines with fmtCADshort(max) and fmtCADshort(min) from lib/maple.ts:4, and a 'Net worth, last 24 months' caption above the chart when not scrubbing. (src/app/(app)/net-worth/hero.tsx:114, src/app/(app)/net-worth/hero.tsx:135) [code]
[P2] Trend chart pointer handlers (onPointerUp / onPointerLeave) | The user drags a thumb across the chart to find, say, last Christmas; the label flips to 'December 2025' and the number changes, but the moment they lift the thumb everything snaps back to 'Today'. They cannot read the past value calmly, screenshot it, or compare it to the tiles. | hero.tsx:124-125 set scrubIdx to null on pointerup and pointerleave. On a phone there is no hover, so the only way to hold a past month is to keep pressing, which also keeps the finger over the dot and part of the curve. The assets/liabilities tiles (page.tsx:130-147) never follow the scrub either, so the split for that month only lives in the 13.5px delta line. | Keep scrubIdx after pointerup (remove the onPointerUp/onPointerLeave resets for pointerType === 'touch'); render a small 'Back to today' ghost button (Button variant ghost, h-11) beside the MapleLabel while a month is pinned, and clear on that tap. Optionally drive the two StatTiles from scrubPoint so the split follows the pinned month. (src/app/(app)/net-worth/hero.tsx:124, src/app/(app)/net-worth/page.tsx:131) [code]
[P2] Trend chart SVG className touch-none | A thumb that lands on the 180px-tall chart to scroll the page instead starts scrubbing; the page does not move. The chart sits mid-screen exactly where a one-thumb scroll begins, so the user has to aim for the card padding or the tiles to scroll past it. | hero.tsx:117 applies touch-none to a full-width 180px block, and onPointerMove scrubs on every touch move (hero.tsx:121-123) with no horizontal-intent check. Page is short today, but with the labels and any future sections this becomes a scroll trap; the same pattern is on the dashboard hero (dashboard/client.tsx:334). | Replace touch-none with touch-pan-y so vertical swipes scroll; in onPointerDown record the start point and only call handleScrub once |dx| > |dy| and |dx| > 8px (or on a 250ms hold). Apply the same to the dashboard chart. (src/app/(app)/net-worth/hero.tsx:117, src/app/(app)/net-worth/hero.tsx:121) [code]
[P2] Page data fetch + empty-state branch | If Supabase times out or RLS rejects a query, the user is shown 'Nothing to chart yet - Add your accounts and import...' with an 'Add an account' button, as though their linked bank accounts had disappeared. Nothing says it was a failure or offers retry. | page.tsx:25-41 destructure only data and never read error; accounts ?? [] at page.tsx:46 and hasData at page.tsx:85 turn any failure into the first-run empty state. For a finance app a wrong 'you have nothing' is a trust break, and the route-level (app)/error.tsx never triggers because nothing throws. | Capture error from each query; if any is set, throw new Error('Could not load net worth') so (app)/error.tsx renders, or render <EmptyState title="Couldn't load your net worth" body="Check your connection and pull down to retry." /> with no 'Add an account' CTA. Keep the current empty state only when all three queries succeeded. (src/app/(app)/net-worth/page.tsx:25, src/app/(app)/net-worth/page.tsx:86) [code]
[P2] Assets / Liabilities StatTiles | The user sees Liabilities $18.2K and wants to know which card or loan that is. The tile does nothing when tapped; the answer is on the balance sheet, which is another More -> Balance sheet trip (2 taps) and a different screen's vocabulary. | page.tsx:131-146 render plain StatTiles with no href. Recognition over recall: a total that cannot be expanded forces the user to remember what accounts they have. | Wrap each StatTile in <Link href="/balance-sheet"> (or /accounts) with className block and add a foot of 'See accounts ->' (12px) so the tile reads as tappable; keep the 44px min height (compact tile is ~70px). Alternatively list the top 3 accounts per side under the tiles with their balances. (src/app/(app)/net-worth/page.tsx:131, src/components/ui/stat-tile.tsx:37) [code]
[P2] Hero figure div (48px serif) | A household with a mortgage sees '−$412,385.20'; at 48px serif inside a card with 24px padding on a 390px screen (about 310px of text width) the figure likely wraps onto two lines or clips, and the tabular digits stop lining up with the label above. | hero.tsx:79-85 has no responsive size step, no truncate/min-w-0 and no compact fallback; 12 glyphs at 48px is ~300-330px. Compare stat-tile.tsx:51 which at least truncates. [P3] EmptyState action button | A user who just linked their bank opens Net worth before the first sync arrives and is told to 'Import transactions', a button that leads to the manual import flow. The product model says the user never imports by hand when Plaid is linked. | Select plaid_item_id in the accounts query; if any account is linked, render body 'Your bank's first sync is on its way - pull down to refresh.' with a secondary Link to /accounts labelled 'See accounts'; keep 'Import transactions' only when no account is Plaid-linked. [P3] 'Today' MapleLabel and 'today' tile hints; no sync timestamp | The figure is labelled 'Today' but a scheduled or future-dated transaction already changes it (it is really 'through the end of this month'), and nothing says when the bank balance was last pulled, so the user cannot tell if a purchase from an hour ago is in the number. | page.tsx:65 uses the current month; accountBalanceAt (balances.ts:78, 103) applies every transaction up to month end. hero.tsx:76 and page.tsx:136/144 say 'Today'/'today'. The hint text is 11px (stat-tile.tsx:58). No last-synced time is fetched. | Pass the latest plaid_items.last_synced_at (or the newest snapshot as_of) into the hero and render 'Today · synced 2h ago' in the MapleLabel; or change the label to 'This month'. FINDINGS: U13:chart-no-period-labels, U13:scrub-clears-on-release, U13:chart-touch-none-blocks-scroll, U13:tiles-not-drillable, U13:hero-overflow-large-negative, U13:today-label-is-month-end, U13:sign-glyph-mixed, U13:tiles-compact-abbrev-unneeded, U13:no-hide-balances, U13:yoy-gate-unexplained, U13:small-secondary-text, U13:scrub-pointer-only, U13:tx-limit-silent-truncation, U13:query-error-shows-empty-state (see parent block)

```
SCREEN: /goals (U14 goals)
USER GOAL(S): See at a glance how far along each savings goal is and how much is left; Add a new savings goal (name + target, optional date/account); Update a goal's progress after money was moved to it; Mark a goal achieved / reopen it; Archive a goal that no longer matters
ELEMENT COUNT: 5 (primary 1, secondary 3, destructive 1, above fold 4) [code-estimate]
TAPS TO PRIMARY GOAL: 2 (scrolls 0) - From a cold /dashboard the Goals widget is NOT in DEFAULT_LAYOUT (dashboard/client.tsx:48) and /goals is in the 'plans' group, not one of the three tab-bar slots (shell.tsx:50,60), so reaching the list is More -> Goals = 2 taps, 0 scrolls (the overall card + first goal card are above the fold). Add goal = More -> Goals -> Add goal -> type name -> type target -> Add goal = 4 taps + 2 required fields, 1 decision (funding account). Update progress = More -> Goals -> Edit -> tap 'Current progress' -> clear prefilled 0.00/old value -> type -> Save = 6 taps + a select-all; it is the most common repeat action here and has no short path, and it asks the user to type a balance the linked funding account already knows.
HEURISTIC SCORE: 8/20 (H1=1 H2=1 H3=1 H4=1 H5=0 H6=2 H7=0 H8=1 H9=0 H10=1)

TAPS TO PRIMARY GOAL: 1 (scrolls 3) - From /dashboard: 1 tap to a bottom-nav/menu 'Loans' entry (not read in this pass - nav component out of scope) lands directly on the household debt rollup with zero further taps or decisions needed to answer 'what do I owe and when am I debt-free' - meets the zero-tap/zero-scroll overview bar for the top of page. Recording a rate change from cold open is 1 tap to /loans + scroll to the loan card + fill 2 required fields + 1 tap Add = effectively 2 taps + 1 scroll + 2 decisions (which month, what rate), within the <=3 tap / <=2 decision budget.

Submit fails with 'Effective month must be the first of a month (YYYY-MM-01).' | type="date" lets the user select any day; the constraint is enforced only after a round-trip, with an error message that leaks the storage format (YYYY-MM-01) instead of plain language. | Change the input to <input type="month" name="effective_month">, which only ever returns a year-month value, then append '-01' server-side before validating/storing. This makes the invalid state unselectable instead of merely rejected. If type="month" support is a concern, at minimum reword the error to 'Pick a month - it always starts on the 1st.' without the ISO token. (src/app/(app)/loans/rate-history.tsx:90, src/app/(app)/loans/actions.ts:64) [code]
[P2] Amortisation schedule table, page.tsx:337-388 | On a 390px phone the 7-column table (#, Rate, Starting, Interest, Principal, Payment, Ending) is wider than the screen; the user must scroll sideways per row to read the interest/principal split they came for, after already seeing a summary 'Next payment split' tile above. | Dense multi-column tabular finance data is hard to scan one-thumb; DataTable correctly keeps the horizontal scroll contained to the table (data-table.tsx:30) so it doesn't break the page, but the content itself is still desktop-shaped. | On narrow viewports, replace the table rows with a compact stacked-card list per month (month #, rate, then a two-line Starting->Ending with interest/principal shown as the existing green/red split used in the 'Next payment split' tile), keeping the table only at sm: breakpoint and above. (src/app/(app)/loans/page.tsx:344) [code]
[P3] Remove rate-change confirm sheet, rate-history.tsx:72-81 | Tapping Remove on a specific rate-change row opens a sheet titled generically 'Remove this rate change?' with no reference to which rate or date is being deleted, so on a list with several changes the user can't visually confirm from the sheet alone that they picked the right row. | Product model requires destructive confirms to name exactly what is being lost; the prompt string is static and doesn't include r.annual_rate_bps or r.effective_month. | Build the prompt per-row, e.g. prompt={`Remove the ${(r.annual_rate_bps/100).toFixed(3)}% change effective ${formatDate(r.effective_month)}?`} in rate-history.tsx:75. (src/app/(app)/loans/rate-history.tsx:75) [code]
[P3] Remove rate-change action, actions.ts:91-99 | After confirming removal of a rate change there is no undo; if the household member picks the wrong row despite the confirm sheet, the only recovery is re-typing the deleted rate change from memory. | Product model favours undoable/logged edits; deleteRateChange performs a hard delete with no soft-delete/undo affordance, unlike other destructive flows in the app that pair confirm with a follow-up undo toast. | Add a brief 'Rate change removed. Undo' toast after deleteRateChange succeeds, backed by re-inserting the same row (id, effective_month, annual_rate_bps, note) if Undo is tapped within a few seconds. (src/app/(app)/loans/actions.ts:91) [code]
[P3] Loan-terms save feedback, form.tsx:76-86 | For an existing loan the terms form lives inside a collapsed <details> disclosure; after tapping Update terms the 'Terms saved.' text appears inline at the bottom of that disclosure, which can sit below the fold on a long loan card with a full schedule, so a one-thumb user scanning back up may not see confirmation happened. | H1 (visibility of system status) is best served by feedback that's guaranteed visible near the action, not dependent on scroll position within a long card. | Pair the inline banner with a brief toast (or auto-scroll the disclosure summary into view on save) so success/error is visible regardless of scroll position. (src/app/(app)/loans/form.tsx:76) [code]
[P2] 'Save terms' button on the loan-terms form (loans/form.tsx submit) | After tapping Save terms, the button flips to a disabled 'Saving…' and stays there for 38+ seconds of active watching, with no spinner, no toast, no progress indicator. The underlying POST /loans request actually completed with a 200 well within the first few seconds (confirmed in the network log), but the client-side UI never reflects it - only a full page reload reveals the terms saved correctly. | This is the same class of hung-save bug documented repeatedly in earlier journeys (category adds, budget edits, transaction adds, rules) - a revalidatePath fan-out or client transition that doesn't settle even though the server-side mutation succeeded almost instantly. On a phone, a user has no way to distinguish this from a genuinely broken save and will very plausibly re-tap, navigate away thinking it failed, or wait far longer than necessary. | Resolve the pending/Saving… client state directly from the server action's own return value/promise resolution rather than relying on Next's revalidation+re-render cycle to settle it - the fix should make the button return to its resting state within roughly 1-2 seconds of the actual server response, matching what the 'Add' rate-change control on this same page already does correctly. (src/app/(app)/loans/actions.ts:49, src/app/(app)/loans/form.tsx:1) [seen live: J12]

(src/app/(app)/transactions/import/wizard.tsx:25) [code]
[P1] Default sign convention ('Auto (respect minus sign)') applied to a pasted CSV | Pasted a 6-row CSV with standard bank-export sign convention (debits negative, e.g. '-184.22' for a Costco purchase; the one credit, an e-transfer, positive '250.00'). The wizard's own live preview showed the correct signs before import ('-$184.22' for Costco, '$250.00' for the e-transfer). After tapping Import, every single row's direction flipped in /transactions: Costco shows '+$184.22' (an inflow), Petro-Canada shows '+$62.40', Amazon shows '+$15.00', and the one genuine inflow, E-TRANSFER RECEIVED, shows '-$250.00' (an outflow). | This is the exact scenario the product exists to support - a statement from a bank Plaid can't reach - and it silently corrupts every row's money direction while claiming success ('Imported 6 transactions.'). A household relying on this path to keep their numbers straight now has a wrong outflow/inflow split and, unless they manually check every row against their real statement, a wrong picture of their spending baked into the same uncategorized pile as their bank-synced rows. | In wizard.tsx, the field comment on line 84 documents the invariant: 'amountCents: number // signed: positive = outflow'. Flip it to `signed = wasNegative ? Math.abs(amountAbs) : -Math.abs(amountAbs)` (matching the sign the manual-direction branch on line 258 already uses correctly), and add a regression test that pastes a negative-amount CSV row and asserts the resulting transaction renders as an outflow with a minus sign after import, not just that amountCents is negative. TAPS TO PRIMARY GOAL: 1 (scrolls 0)

FINDINGS
[P1] Reload button on the 'Update ready' toast | A household member is mid-edit somewhere in the app (e.g. typing a split note or a manual transaction) when a deploy lands and the update toast appears; if they (or a second device sharing the SW cache) tap Reload, the page reloads immediately with no warning, silently discarding any unsaved input. | sw-registrar.tsx's reload() posts SKIP_WAITING and the page reloads on controllerchange with no check for pending user input anywhere in the app; update-toast.tsx's Reload button has no confirmation step. | Before calling waiting.postMessage in sw-registrar.tsx's reload(), check a shared 'has unsaved changes' flag (e.g. from the split editor / add-transaction form) and show a one-line confirm ('You have unsaved changes - reload anyway?') only when it's true; otherwise reload immediately as today. (src/components/pwa/sw-registrar.tsx:438, src/components/pwa/update-toast.tsx:630) [code]
[P2] 'Update ready' toast (update-toast.tsx) | Once a new worker is waiting, the pill renders indefinitely above the tab bar with only a Reload action; a user who wants to finish their current task first has no way to dismiss it and it will keep reappearing on every render until they reload. | UpdateToast takes only onReload/busy props; there is no dismiss handler, no close button, and no Escape-key listener (contrast with IOSInstallHint which has both). | Add a small 'Later' dismiss control (or tap-outside/Escape) to UpdateToast that hides the pill for the rest of the session (in-memory state in ServiceWorkerRegistrar, not persisted) without cancelling the pending update - it should reappear next visit if still waiting. (src/components/pwa/update-toast.tsx:617, src/components/pwa/sw-registrar.tsx:446) [code]
[P2] Service worker registration | If navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js') rejects (blocked by browser policy, storage quota, private mode, etc.), the only trace is a console.warn - the user sees no difference and never learns why install, offline fallback, and push notifications aren't working for them. | .catch((err) => console.warn(...)) is the entire failure path; there is no state exposed to the UI to show a banner or retry affordance. | On registration failure, set a small module-level/exported flag (or context) that IOSInstallHint and any future 'enable push' UI can read to explain 'this browser can't install Maple' instead of silently doing nothing. (src/components/pwa/sw-registrar.tsx:423) [code]
[P2] Service worker offline handling | The branded offline.html only ever shows on a hard/full page navigation. If a user loses connectivity while already inside the installed app and taps a normal in-app link (client-side App Router navigation, which fetches the RSC payload), that fetch is deliberately excluded from SW handling and just fails - the user gets whatever Next's default client-side fetch-error behavior is, not the reassuring 'You're offline / Try again' screen. | isNextRouterRequest() (sw.js:165-175) returns true for RSC/prefetch requests and the fetch handler returns early for them (sw.js:129-131) before any caching logic runs; only req.mode === 'navigate' gets networkFirstPage's offline.html fallback (sw.js:149-154). ## Part 3 - Final report

Sections 1 and 1b (the ranked top 10 and the headline metrics) are in Part 0 above.
Findings after merging duplicates: 99 P1, 208 P2, 186 P3; 114 of them are steps the product model says should not exist.

### 2. Cross-screen inconsistencies (same concept, different label, placement or behaviour)

- The balance of an account: the dashboard card shows the live balance with no label, the /accounts row shows the opening balance under an "OPENING" caption ($0.00 for every linked account), the /accounts header tiles show live compact totals ("$123.5K"), and the balance sheet prefills the start-of-month anchor rather than the derived balance (J1b, J15, U07, U12).
- Sign of money: transaction rows use a separate "-"/"+" span that is hidden from screen readers; stat tiles are unsigned and colour-only; the dashboard "Saved" tile uses `sign="always"`; liabilities are "-$4,321.00" on /accounts but "$4,321.00" in red on the dashboard card; the /transactions Net tile is computed as outflow minus inflow and coloured green when positive (U01, U03, J1).
- Save feedback: budgets show "✓ Saved" inline for 2.5 s; the add-transaction sheet just closes; sharing a row toasts; adding an account, a category or a goal shows nothing; splits print "Splits saved." in a 12.5 px paragraph inside the form; import prints a status line on the page (U06, U05, U10, U07, U08, U04, U19).
- Confirmation: `ConfirmModal` for most destructive actions, a hand-rolled confirm for deleting a rule (`rules/rules-list.tsx:227`), a `Sheet` for closing a settlement period (`shared/period-card.tsx:83`), and no confirmation for revoking an invitation or unarchiving (U09, U10, U18, U07).
- Back affordance: a top-left chevron on 4 routes (the import screens and /categories), a text link on 5, nothing on 17; no swipe-back anywhere (U00 and every unit).
- Month navigation: a top MonthNav on 6 screens, none on the dashboard, an inline year select on /contributions (U03, U06, U10, U11, U12, U17 vs U02, U15).
- Timestamps: relative ("synced 3 h ago") on the Setup bank card, raw ISO slices ("2026-08-29 06:16") on the plaid-setup page (U18 vs U21, J14).
- Sync entry points: on /transactions the More-actions "Set up sync" leads to the Gmail auto-import tutorial even when a bank is linked; the real "Sync now" lives four taps away under Setup, Bank connections, Manage bank sync (J7).
- Field labels are 10, 10.5, 11 or 12 px depending on the screen; tile values are anything from 14 to 28 px (patterns data across 26 units).
- Sheet dismissal: X plus scrim plus Esc on 12 sheets, no scrim on one, no X on another; a grab handle is drawn on all of them but none swipe (U00, `components/ui/sheet.tsx:113`).
- Pending states: some buttons say "Saving…", some "Adding…", some dim, the filter "Show results" and "Clear" show nothing at all until the server answers (U03, J15).

### 3. Flows to shorten (current path versus proposed path)

| Flow | Today | Proposed |
|---|---|---|
| Categorize one incoming transaction (the daily job) | Cold open, Transactions tab, tap "Categorize" on the row, tap a chip = 3 taps, or More…, open picker, scroll, pick, Done, Apply = 6 for a category outside the six chips; then wait 2-3 s with no visible result | Cold open shows "19 to categorize" under the greeting; tap it (or the tab), tap the chip already visible on the row = 2 taps; row updates instantly; long-tail categories through a type-ahead list |
| Split out GST | Splits, Add split, category picker, type 5% amount, retype the first row, Save = 6 taps plus two mental calculations | Splits, "Split out GST (5%)" = 2 taps |
| Notice and fix a broken bank link | Invisible on every daily screen; More, Setup, Manage bank sync, Re-authenticate = 4 taps if you know where to look; the fix then does not hold | Dashboard banner and a badge on the account rows, "Reconnect" = 1 tap, sync runs after Link and the pill clears |
| Log a cash purchase from any screen | From /budgets: "+", route change to /transactions (3 s), sheet, amount, account, category, description, Save, then a tap back = 8 taps plus a wait | "+" opens the sheet over the current screen everywhere (register a quick-add target in the shell, `lib/quick-add.tsx:27-43`), account = last used, recents first, Save pinned = 4 taps, no wait, no return |
| Add a category | More, Setup, three drags down 3544 px to the Categories card, name, Add = 3 taps + 3 scrolls; the new row lands 1133 px below the form | "+ New" inside any category picker (already exists on transaction rows) and a Categories tile in the More sheet = 2 taps, new row visible |
| Move $50 between two budgets | Open Food, compute 400-50, retype, open Coffee, compute 70+50, retype, Save = 5 taps plus arithmetic; the hero total then drops to the parent-only sum | "Move" action on a row: pick the destination, type 50, Save = 3 taps, live total |
| Sync now from the Transactions tab | More actions, "Set up sync" (lands on the Gmail tutorial), back, More, Setup, Manage bank sync, Sync now = 6 taps | Pull-to-refresh that prints "+3 new · 0 enriched" when it finishes, and "Sync now" in More actions whenever a bank is linked = 1 gesture |
| Invite a partner who cannot receive email | Invite, read the provider's error, copy the link; the pending row then offers only Revoke, so a lost link cannot be re-sent | Invite shows a branded "Share this link" card with Copy; the Invited row has "Copy link" and "Resend" next to Revoke |
| Settle up with the partner | More, Shared (the owes line is at the top), then four screens of scrolling past 17 share rows to "Record a payment by hand", which arrives prefilled; Save then sits on "Saving…" 10 s or more | "Settle up" button on the owes line opens the same prefilled form in a sheet; Save resolves from the action result with "Recorded $42.11 · All square" |
| See who owes whom across accounts | /shared shows household-wide totals but lists transactions for one source account at a time (17 options, defaulting to the first credit card) | "All accounts" as the default source, the account filter as a refinement |
| First run with a bank | Household (2 inputs), bank step with the manual form focused and Connect under the keyboard, "Skip for now" ends onboarding and lands on a $0 net-worth chart | Household, one tall "Connect your bank" button with "add manually" underneath, skip goes to step 3, the first dashboard says "Your bank brought in 48 transactions · 19 to categorize" |

### 4. Elements to delete app-wide

- The step-through "Review" triage modal as the primary categorization path (`transactions/uncategorized-review.tsx:124-256`): keep in-place chips, keep "apply to similar" as a row action, and at most leave a low-key "Review one by one" link.
- The "needs a title" rule inside the uncategorized queue (`transactions/page.tsx:201`, `lib/title.ts:141`) and the "or title" wording of the pill.
- "OPENING" as the number shown on account rows (`accounts/row.tsx:172-179`).
- Editable balance inputs for Plaid-linked accounts in the balance-sheet form (`balance-sheet/form.tsx:126`) and the typed "Current progress" field when a goal has a funding account (`goals/add-form.tsx:54`).
- The "Categorize" reveal toggle on uncategorized rows (`transactions/row.tsx:251-264`).
- Environment-variable names and "restart the dev server" copy inside user-facing pages (`transactions/import/plaid-setup/page.tsx:71-100`).
- The "Hands-free" Gmail teaser card on the CSV import page for households with a linked bank (J13).
- The dashboard's 1M/3M/YTD/1Y/ALL range pills and flat-line chart when the household has no accounts (J0); replace with the connect-a-bank card.
- Raw ISO timestamps on the plaid-setup page (`plaid-wizard.tsx:186`); use the relative phrasing the Setup card already has.
- The `src/lib/maple.ts` money formatters (dead code with a different minus sign) before anyone wires them in.

### 4b. Elements to move into the thumb zone (bottom third of a 390x844 screen)

- The primary button of every form sheet (Add transaction, Add goal, rule editor, record payment, update balances) into the `Sheet` footer slot, sized by `visualViewport` so it sits above the keyboard.
- "Save splits" and "Add split" (currently covered by the keyboard and the tab bar at the moment they are needed).
- "Save budgets" (make the bar truly sticky above the tab bar by removing `overflow-x: hidden` from `body`, `globals.css:361`).
- "Add account", "Invite", "Create household", "Connect a bank" (all measured under the tab bar or the keyboard, or 1206 px down).
- "Sign out" and the Settings gear (top-right on every screen, y = 1015 in the More sheet) into the first row of the More sheet.
- The uncategorized pill's "Review" action and the dashboard inbox card: place the card directly under the greeting so the first tap is within reach of the thumb, and give the Transactions tab a count badge.
- Back: add swipe-back on every screen with a chevron, or a bottom-anchored back in the tab bar row on the import screens.

### 5. Patterns worth standardizing (the one place the app does it well)

- Optimistic, instant feedback: category rename updates the row on the client before the server answers (J16). Apply to categorize, share toggle, filter changes, account switcher and delete.
- The Filter sheet footer ("Clear all / Show results", 44 px rows, pinned) is the template for every sheet's primary action.
- The import result sentence ("Imported 6 transactions, enriched 6 existing, skipped 0 duplicates") is the template for pull-to-refresh, Sync now and Link & sync, which today print nothing or "OK · +48 new".
- ConfirmModal copy that names the loss and offers the secondary choice (delete rule with "Also unshare the 6 transactions this rule shared") is the template for the rule-list hand-rolled confirm, the close-period sheet and revoke-invite.
- In-place expansion under the row (Splits) is the template for edit and categorize; no modal for a one-field decision.
- The Setup bank card's relative timestamps and "One bank needs attention" sentence are the template for a dashboard banner.
- The prefilled "Record a payment by hand" form (from, to, amount, date all correct before the user touches it) is the template for every form that can know its answer: add transaction (last account, recent category), budgets (last month's number), goals (funding account balance).
- The invite landing copy ("Your own accounts stay private; the household sees only joint accounts and the transactions you share") is the template for the privacy language the app lacks elsewhere.

### Reported bug - bank re-authentication (reproduced live in J14, root cause in code)

Repro (sandbox, local production build of commit `f78bff5`, household "E2E household" with the Tartan-Dominion item linked):

1. Force the item into `ITEM_LOGIN_REQUIRED` with Plaid's `/sandbox/item/reset_login` (script `reset-login.mjs`, printed `status_before = active`).
2. Cold open /dashboard, then /accounts, then /transactions: no banner, no badge, no pill mentions the bank; the 14 linked rows still read "$0.00 Opening"; the transactions pill talks about categories only.
3. Pull to refresh on /transactions: "Release to sync", "Syncing…", back to "Pull to sync"; no result text.
4. Open /transactions/import/plaid-setup: the pill now reads "Re-auth needed" and the Recent syncs log shows "2026-08-29 06:16 · Re-auth needed · +0 new · 0 enriched"; /setup's bank card says "One bank needs attention."
5. Tap "Sync now" on that bank: alert "No connected banks to sync." with the bank listed one line above.
6. Tap "Re-authenticate", complete update-mode Link (Tartan, `user_good` / `pass_good`), Finish: the page prints "Re-authenticated. Syncing…".
7. Reload: pill still "Re-auth needed"; "Sync now" twice more: "No connected banks to sync." both times; zero new rows in Recent syncs.

Why (verified by reading the code):

- `plaid_items.status` becomes `login_required` only when a sync runs and Plaid answers `ITEM_LOGIN_REQUIRED` (`src/lib/plaid-sync.ts:208-211`) or the webhook lands (`src/app/api/plaid/webhook/route.ts:120`); the only automatic sync is the daily cron, so a broken item stays "Active" until someone pulls.
- The status is rendered only by `setup/bank-connections.tsx:29-49` and `plaid-wizard.tsx:199-231`; nothing on /dashboard, /accounts or /transactions reads it.
- `triggerPlaidSync` (`src/app/(app)/transactions/import/plaid-setup/actions.ts:426-433`) builds `.eq('household_id', ...).in('status', ['active','error'])` and then ANDs `.eq('id', itemRowId)`; a `login_required` row never matches, so a targeted sync returns `{ error: 'No connected banks to sync.' }`.
- After a successful update-mode Link nothing writes status back to `active`; the only place that happens is a successful `syncPlaidItem` (`src/lib/plaid-sync.ts:675-679`).
- `usePlaidReauth.onSuccess` (`src/components/plaid/plaid-connect.tsx:424-430`) awaits `triggerPlaidSync(id)`, never inspects the result, and calls `onDone` unconditionally; the wizard's `onDone` prints "Re-authenticated. Syncing…" and refreshes.
- The same hook drives `/plaid/oauth-return`, so OAuth banks hit the identical dead end.

Minimal fix (`actions.ts:426-433`):

```ts
let query = service
  .from('plaid_items')
  .select('id, household_id, item_id, cursor, status, last_synced_at')
  .eq('household_id', ctx.householdId)
if (itemRowId) {
  query = query.eq('id', itemRowId)
} else {
  query = query.in('status', ['active', 'error'])
}
```

Hardening: check the `triggerPlaidSync` result in `usePlaidReauth.onSuccess` and route a failure to `onError` with a real message; when the item exists but is excluded by status, return "This bank needs re-authentication" instead of "No connected banks to sync."; surface `login_required`, `error` and `revoked` on /dashboard and on the linked account rows with a one-tap "Reconnect".

### Other bugs found on the way (not UX polish, real defects)

- Pending states stall after the server has answered: categories "Adding…" stuck over 398 s on production (POST 200 `{ok:true}`), "Link & sync" 40 s, rule creation over 58 s (the rule was created and applied to 9 rows), add transaction 48 s and 18 s, loan terms over 38 s, record payment 10 s or more with the payment saved (J2, J1b, J3, J8, J12, J10). The service worker ignores non-GET requests (`public/sw.js:77`), so it is not the cause. Candidates: the six-route `revalidate()` in `transactions/actions.ts:26-33` re-rendering a `force-dynamic` dashboard with 13 queries inside the action response, or the experimental view transition flag. Discriminating checks are listed in the top 10, item 3.
- In-place categorization returns 200 but the row does not update (J3, J7): the client keeps the stale list until a reload.
- "Add goal" does nothing in the production bundle (J12, J16); `goals/add-form.tsx:18` calls `onSaved()` on mount, which closes the sheet in the same tick (`goal-controls.tsx:40`).
- Opening balance "12.505" is accepted by the form and saved as $0.00 without any message (J1; parseForm in `accounts/actions.ts` treats an unparsable amount as 0).
- Typing "12,50" into the add-transaction amount is rewritten to "1250" as you type (`add-form.tsx:95`).
- The uncategorized pill only counts the month in view; 15 July rows (83% of that month) were invisible from August (J3).
- "Over by $X" in red appears on every category that has no budget at all (J6, `budgets/table.tsx:317-323`).
- Two identical Uber rules could be created and a paused rule is visually identical to an active one (J7, J16).
- Searching "SQ" finds nothing because the cleaned title replaces the raw bank descriptor (J15).
- The invite result exposes the email provider's raw error including an email address (J9a; local environment used the sandbox sender, but the message path is the same in production when Resend rejects a recipient).
